Noclip: cast between consecutive samples, both ends raised 3 studs (`RaycastParams` Include on anchored world geometry), counting only wall-like faces (absolute `result.Normal.Y` under 0.5) facing the ray, so crests, ramps and landings never count; 3 counts in 30 s is a violation. On foot, one 2 Hz loop flags horizontal speed above 2.5x `WalkSpeed` or 4 s airborne without descending over 12 studs up. Exempt: server teleport tokens, chairlift rides, ragdoll, the foam pit and water.

[473] Responses escalate on a per-player score decaying 1 point per 60 s. Every violation is corrected per [75]. At 6 points the player is flagged: raw DataStore `ACFlags_v1` (key UserId, newest 50 entries with rule, values, ping and position) records it and online admins are notified. At 15 points the server kicks with "Removed for abnormal movement data. If this was a mistake, just rejoin." `Players:BanAsync` is reserved for confirmed cases: 3 kicks for one rule within 24 h plus an admin review of the flags. Config: `UserIds`, `ApplyToUniverse = true`, `Duration = 604800` (7 days; -1 after a second review), `DisplayReason`, `PrivateReason`, `ExcludeAltAccounts = false`. Honeypot-free: no decoy remotes, trap parts or bait values, which cause unreviewable false bans. Acceptance test: 20 hours of legitimate riding at 300 ms simulated lag yields zero kicks.

[474] Live admin tools are separate from the Studio-only DevTools of [45]. An `AdminCommands` ModuleScript under AntiCheatService (no extra Service, so the canon boot order stands) reads a server-only `AdminAllowlist` ModuleScript beside it, `{[UserId] = "Owner" | "Moderator"}`, never group rank or display name. The server clones `ServerStorage/ServerAssets/AdminPanel` into PlayerGui only for allowlisted players, and every `Admin_Command` call re-checks allowlist and rank. Moderators: kick, spectate, view flags, `Players:GetBanHistoryAsync`, ban and `Players:UnbanAsync` with a required reason, and `Announce` broadcasts. Owners add cash or vehicle grants (reason `Admin`, max $1,000,000 per action), server shutdown and allowlist reload. Each action writes {T, AdminId, Command, TargetId, Args, JobId} to raw DataStore `AdminAudit_v1` (one key per UTC day, `UpdateAsync` append, 1,000 entries) plus the target's Ledger. Acceptance test: a non-allowlisted `Admin_Command` does nothing, scores 3 points and logs one WARN.

[475] Server frame budget: at 60 Hz the server step averages under 8 ms, with peaks under 14 ms, for all 17 Services combined (server MicroProfiler, Developer Console server jobs). Never give each player a `Heartbeat`, `PostSimulation` or `while true` loop: vehicles share the single [75] sampler, on-foot checks run in one 2 Hz loop over all players, DataService adds no loops because ProfileStore autosaves, and crew refresh, presence writes and pass rechecks share one `task.wait(1)` loop with staggered player buckets; everything else is event-driven. Scheduled jobs stop at 1 ms per frame via `os.clock()` and resume next frame. Web calls never run inside frame callbacks, and global writes check `DataStoreService:GetRequestBudgetForRequestType` first. Acceptance test: 30 players with spawned vehicles and a live race hold server heartbeat at 55+ Hz for 10 minutes ([476]-[500]).

## SECTION 21 - PERFORMANCE, TESTING, LAUNCH & THE BUILD ROADMAP (Paragraphs 476-500)

[476] You will hit these performance targets and prove them by measurement in Phase 13, never by estimate. Frame rate: a steady 60 FPS on a mid-range PC (GTX 1060-class GPU, quad-core CPU, 8 GB RAM) and 30+ FPS on a three-year-old mid-range phone, measured while riding through Harbor City Downtown at night in rain with 10 vehicles in view. Frame budget: 16.7 ms on PC and 33.3 ms on mobile, with all of our Luau combined under 6 ms on mobile and `VehicleController` alone under 1.0 ms. Client memory: 1,000-1,200 MB maximum on low-end mobile, read from `Stats:GetTotalMemoryUsageMb()`. Network: client receive under 50 KB/s in a full 30-player server, aiming for 20-30 KB/s. Server: heartbeat stable at 60 Hz, never below 55 for more than 1 s, and server memory under 3,000 MB.

[477] Workspace streaming properties are Studio-only: the user sets them in the Properties panel during Phase 1 setup ([21]) and no script ever assigns them. Values: `StreamingEnabled` true, `StreamingTargetRadius` 1024, `StreamingMinRadius` 256, `StreamOutBehavior` Opportunistic, `StreamingIntegrityMode` MinimumRadiusPause. Vehicle templates, buildings, gas stations and prop clusters use `ModelStreamingMode` Atomic so nothing arrives half-built; when the server spawns a player's vehicle it sets that clone to PersistentPerPlayer before parenting it and calls `Model:AddPersistentPlayer(owner)`, so it stays Atomic for everyone else; never mark large models Persistent. Client code tolerates missing instances through CollectionService `GetInstanceAddedSignal`/`GetInstanceRemovedSignal`, never an unbounded `WaitForChild`. Before every teleport, garage spawn and race countdown the server calls `Player:RequestStreamAroundAsync(target, 5)` inside pcall and only then pivots; for riders above 150 km/h it requests the point 2 s ahead along their velocity once per second.

[478] Profiling: open the MicroProfiler (Ctrl+Alt+F6) and wrap every per-frame loop in `debug.profilebegin`/`debug.profileend` labels named `<Module>.<Function>` (for example `VehicleController.Step`) so every millisecond is attributable; pause on spikes and look for frames over 33 ms, physics steps dominated by collision-hull contacts, and render time driven by shadows or particles. Developer Console (F9): the Memory tab must show `LuaHeap` and instance counts flat within 5% over 10 minutes of riding (growth means a missing `Trove` cleanup), the Network tab must show no remote above its `NetDefs` `Rate`, and the Scripts tab no script above 3% activity. The Studio Script Profiler records client and server CPU per function; anything over 0.5 ms per frame is optimized first. A `/perf` overlay (a DevTools command in Studio, an AdminPanel toggle for `AdminAllowlist` users in live servers, [451]-[475]) shows FPS, `Stats.DataReceiveKbps`, memory and `workspace:GetRealPhysicsFPS()`.

[479] Optimization rules, part one. Pool instead of churning: tire-mark segments (prewarm 128), particle bursts, traffic cars, pedestrian rigs, Sound instances, checkpoint markers and UI list rows come from pools; no `Instance.new` or `:Destroy()` runs in per-frame code, and after Phase 7 no `UnionOperation` remains in `Workspace/World` ([294]). Loading: `ReplicatedFirst/Loading.client.luau`, the one sanctioned exception to [23] and [25], calls `ReplicatedFirst:RemoveDefaultLoadingScreen()` and shows its own `ScreenGui` (DisplayOrder 100) with tips and progress fed by `ContentProvider:PreloadAsync` in pcall: UI atlases and fonts, then owned vehicles' templates and engine loops, then spawn-region decals. It fades over 0.4 s once `game:IsLoaded()`, `ServerReady` ([25]) and the first character spawn (after the server's [477] stream request, [462]) are true, or after 25 s regardless, preloads continuing. Acceptance test: a cold join on a mid-range phone rides within 20 s with no pop-in at the spawn plaza.

[480] Optimization rules, part two. Lights: only the local player's headlight casts shadows; other vehicle lights and every `StreetLight` use `Shadows` false and `Range` of 20 studs or less, and a client culler enables at most 24 Light instances within 300 studs of the camera (12 on mobile). `CastShadow` is false on foliage and props under 2 studs. Particles: total visible emission under 1,200 particles/s on PC and 400 on mobile, 40/s per vehicle, emitters off beyond 200 studs. Update tiers by camera distance: Tier 0, the local vehicle, every frame; Tier 1 under 150 studs at 30 Hz with animation, IK and audio; Tier 2 at 150-500 studs at 10 Hz without IK; Tier 3 beyond 500 studs at 2 Hz, silent and unanimated. Lower quality levels scale per [321]-[340].

[481] Testing, part one. Every phase ends with Play solo plus Studio Test > Clients and Servers: 2 clients for Phases 1-7 ([12]), 4 for Phases 8-13, reading server and client Output. Verify each client sees the other's vehicle animating, ownership hands over on enter and exit, and no remote throws. Use the Device emulator for 16:9 and 19.5:9 phones, a 4:3 tablet and the 1080p console view; check thumb reach, safe-area insets and the [431]-[450] text floors (14 px touch body, 24 px 10-foot). Gamepad: with a real Xbox or PlayStation controller, complete onboarding, a job, a purchase and a race without the mouse, menus driven by GuiService selection. Lag: set Studio Settings > Network > Incoming Replication Lag to 0.25 s and repeat purchases, races and enter/exit; nothing may double-charge, desync or snap more than 2 studs.

[482] Testing, part two. Pure modules (`Units`, tire model, gear and RPM math, PI formula, economy payouts, profile migrations) get unit tests as `ServerScriptService/Server/Tests/<Module>.spec` ModuleScripts in TestEZ-style `describe`/`it`/`expect` form (Jest-Lua is fine), run only by DevTools `/selftest` ([45]); for example `Units` must convert 100 km/h to 99.21 studs/s and 21.952 kg to 1 RMU. Studio runtime assertions: no NaN or infinite velocity, suspension compression inside 0..travel, client `Vehicle_State` packets exactly the [36] 10 bytes, Trove empty after despawn. The server's `ScriptContext.Error` handler logs message, trace and script through `Logger`, once per unique message per minute; clients forward theirs through a `Log_ClientError` NetDefs remote, capped at 5 per minute and 300 characters. Check Creator Dashboard error reports after every publish. Phase gate: the [12] standard, 10 minutes with zero errors and no warnings beyond the single [13] placeholder-asset summary.

[483] Launch checklist, part one. Complete the [469] questionnaire first. Store page: a 512x512 icon and up to 10 1920x1080 thumbnails rendered in photo mode from final models, never placeholders or real logos (golden-hour street bike in Harbor City, airborne dirt bike at Throttle Park, Frostline 850 on Summit Snowfield); a description with a two-line hook, features, controls, devices and only fictional brands. Devices: enable Computer, Phone, Tablet and Console only after each passes [481], consoles also the [448] gamepad-only run. Private beta: 10-20 testers for 7-14 days via a group role with play access, reporting via an in-phone Feedback app attaching position, VehicleId, build version and the last 20 `Logger` lines; crash and data bugs block launch. Soft launch: public without sponsored ads for 7 days, then ads only once D1 retention reaches 20% and average sessions 12 minutes.

[484] Launch checklist, part two. Analytics: the eight [449] onboarding steps, `LogEconomyEvent` on every Cash source and sink ([365]) and `LogCustomEvent` for race entries, crashes per minute, rentals and spawns per VehicleId; weekly, compare D1/D7 retention, session length and the [345] bands to targets, tuning `Config` values, never formulas. Releases: a weekly patch and a content update every 3-4 weeks, with notes in the What's New card and description and a version string like `v1.4.2` in the Settings footer and every `Logger` error. Ship with Creator Hub's Migrate To Latest Update; otherwise `game:BindToClose` shows a 5 s notice, ends profiles ([452]) and teleports players to a reserved holding server that returns them to fresh ones. Schema changes ship only with migrations passing every prior-version fixture ([455]). Record the place version before each publish for rollback; keep weekly `.rbxl` backups.

[485] Phase 1 - Foundation. Deliver `Main.server.luau` and `Main.client.luau` per [24]-[25]; the Shared modules `Units`, `Types`, `Signal`, `Trove`, `Net`, `NetDefs`, `Logger` and `Util`; `ConfigValidator`; typed `Config/` stubs; DevTools ([45]); all 17 Services and 12 Controllers as stubs exposing `:Init()` and `:Start()`; and a working `DataService` on ProfileStore. In Studio the user publishes the place, enables Studio Access to API Services, creates the folder tree, sets `Workspace.Gravity` 35, `StreamingEnabled` with the [477] values and the other [21] properties, and inserts ProfileStore at `ServerScriptService/Server/Packages`. Acceptance test: in Play solo and a 2-client run, Output logs every Service and Controller Init then Start in canonical order, `ReplicatedStorage/Remotes` holds every `NetDefs` remote, a wrong Gravity logs exactly one WARN, and a new profile shows $2,500 and the Kurb Street BMX after a rejoin. Relies on Part 1 ([1]-[45]) and Part 10 ([451]-[475]).

[486] Phase 2 - Physics core and the Volt Wasp X. Deliver `Config/Vehicles/volt_wasp_x.luau`; `VehicleService` with `Vehicle_Spawn`, `Vehicle_Exit`, `SetNetworkOwner` on enter, reclaim on exit and the 1.15x-for-2-s speed check; `VehicleController` running spherecast suspension, slip-curve tires, the friction circle and roll-only PD balance in `PreSimulation`; `InputController` bindings; a chase camera; a flat-torque drive stub that Phase 3 replaces. In Studio the user builds a `PhysicsTestPad` Model at the origin with Asphalt, Grass, Sand and Ice strips, a 15-degree ramp and a 30 m (107-stud) radius circle. Acceptance test: 75 km/h top speed within 3% on asphalt; at 40 km/h on the circle lean settles within 2 degrees of 22.8 degrees; weight back plus throttle wheelies; hard front braking stoppies; overleaning on ice crashes; the second client sees smooth motion. Relies on Parts 2-3 ([46]-[75], [101]-[150]).

[487] Phase 3 - Powertrains and audio core. Deliver the electric model (torque curve, current limit, battery sag), the gas model (torque curves, clutch slip, gearbox, CVT, 2T/4T character, limiter, stall), pedal cadence, rider aids, configs for the Kensei K2-250, Ryujin RX-250F, Gopher 200 and Kurb Street BMX, layered RPM crossfades in `AudioController`, and `Vehicle_State` per [36] (10-byte `buffer` packets on an `UnreliableRemoteEvent`, relayed at 10-15 Hz). The user pastes licensed engine audio IDs into `Config/SoundIds`; 0 IDs stay silent. Acceptance test: K2-250 peak power lands inside 7,000-10,500 rpm; the RX-250F cuts at 13,500; a Sim Mode first-gear clutch release at idle without throttle stalls; E and Q shifts never hang; crossfades have no audible step; relayed `Vehicle_State` packets stay under 900 bytes. Relies on Part 2 ([76]-[100]), Part 6 ([251]-[270]) and the Parts 3-4 stat blocks.

[488] Phase 4 - Full roster, VehicleBuilder, garage and dealers. Deliver all 53 `Config/Vehicles/<VehicleId>.luau` files plus the 3-Rail Bike Trailer and Hitch Bike Carrier; `VehicleBuilder` placeholders with every canonical attachment; the 4-wheel, snowmobile and jet ski variants; `ShopService` (`Shop_Buy` with price and Rep gates); `GarageService` (slots, spawning, 60% resale); basic dealership screens at `Dealership` tags. In Studio the user adds a 6,000-stud straight with asphalt, dirt, snow and water lanes to `PhysicsTestPad`. Acceptance test: a debug sweep spawns every VehicleId on its native lane, asserts required attachments, runs full throttle until speed plateaus, logs motorized top speeds within 5% of `TopSpeed_kmh` and despawns leaving no orphans; a Ryujin Gremlin 125 bought at L6 costs $5,500 and resells for $3,300; the 3-slot Studio Garage refuses a fourth vehicle. Relies on Parts 3-5 ([126]-[230]), Part 8 ([341]-[365]) and Part 9 ([431]-[450]).

[489] Phase 5 - Rider animation, crashes, damage and wear. Deliver `RiderAnimationController` blending ride, tuck, lean and wheelie poses via `Animator:LoadAnimation`, with `IKControl` pinning hands to `GripL`/`GripR` and feet to `PegL`/`PegR`; crash thresholds; ragdoll, recovery and the B bail; per-component damage; tire, chain, pad and battery wear; Throttle Works repairs with Mechanic hooks; all persisted per [454]. The user authors animations and pastes IDs into `Config/AssetIds`; 0 IDs fall back to procedural IK poses. Acceptance test: hands stay within 0.05 studs of the grips at full lean and weight shift ([228]); a 60 km/h wall impact ragdolls the rider with no blood; Get Up appears 3 s after ejection and F remounts ([240]-[241]); damage survives a rejoin; two clients meeting head-on at 30 km/h each crash identically everywhere; a Passive rider passes through ([249]). Relies on Parts 5 and 2 ([206]-[250], [46]-[75]).

[490] Phase 6 - World blockout, road graph and streaming. Deliver blockout geometry and terrain for all ten regions at the canon centers; Highway 9, Interstate 90 with its Pinecrest foothills tunnel, and Canyon Road; `Shared/RoadGraph.luau` parsing the [282] `Workspace/RoadGraph` nodes and edges; every CollectionService tag placed (`GasPump`, `ChargingStation`, `JobPickup`, `JobDropoff`, `StreetLight`, `TrafficLight`, `Checkpoint`, `Dealership`, `GaragePoint`); a basic `MapController`; the teleport helper from [477]. In Studio the user re-checks the Phase 1 streaming values from [477], sculpts terrain with the Terrain Editor, sets `Terrain.Decoration` and moves `PhysicsTestPad` into `ServerStorage`. Acceptance test: a Highway 9 loop in the Hauler HD and an Old Town (0, 15, 0) to Summit Snowfield ride on the Volt Wasp X with no fall-through, no unloaded holes ahead and memory inside [476]. Relies on Part 6 ([271]-[295]) and Part 7 ([296]-[320]).

[491] Phase 7 - World art pass, lighting, weather and VFX. Deliver MeshPart props replacing the blockout within the pipeline limits (20,000 triangles, 1024x1024 textures, shared meshes for instancing); lighting and post-processing presets; `TimeService` with the 48-minute day; `WeatherService` broadcasting state and wetness w; `WeatherFXController` for rain, fog, snow and puddles; pooled tire marks and dust, mud and spray particles; quality scaling. In Studio the user confirms `Lighting.Technology` is Future and imports Blender meshes with the 3D Importer. Acceptance test: an in-game day lasts 48 minutes within 10 s; at w = 1 the debug overlay reads asphalt peak grip 0.70 and packed dirt 0.48; Harbor City Downtown at night in heavy rain with 10 vehicles in view holds the [476] targets on PC and a real mid-range phone. Relies on Part 7 ([296]-[340]) and Part 5 ([206]-[230]).

[492] Phase 8 - Economy, jobs and properties. Deliver `EconomyService` (server-only Cash changes with reason codes); `JobService` running Food Delivery, Parcel Courier, Parts Runner, Moto-Taxi, Bike Transport and Mechanic; `Config/Economy` and `Config/Jobs`; GPS routing on the road graph; Pumpline fuel ($2.00/L) and VoltCharge ($0.40/kWh) with 10x time compression; all four properties; the `PhoneController` jobs app. The user places `JobPickup` and `JobDropoff` parts per [341]-[365]. Acceptance test: with onboarding skipped, 15 minutes of Food Delivery on the starter BMX pays $850-1,150 (the [346] $4,000/hour rate); an empty Volt Wasp X charges to full at 20 kW (tapering above 80%) in about 56 s for $0.96; buying the Maple Heights House ($120,000, L10) raises garage capacity from 3 to 9; a forged `Job_Action` completion pays $0; Cash survives a rejoin in a 4-client test. Relies on Part 8 ([341]-[365]) and Part 10 ([451]-[475]).

[493] Phase 9 - Customization, tuning, dyno and PI. Deliver `CustomizationService`; `Config/Upgrades` trees per powertrain; tire and suspension parts; tuning sliders for gearing, preload, damping, tire pressure and power maps; `DynoSim` for the three [382] dyno rooms; the PI formula (100-999, classes D-X); chrome, chameleon and satin finishes gated by Pro Paint Lab; visual parts; rider gear; saved builds with filtered names. In Studio the user positions the [382] dyno rollers and the Port Warehouse paint booth. Acceptance test: server and client compute identical PI; each upgrade moves PI exactly as the formula predicts; the stock Volt Wasp X rates PI 375 +/- 30 ([385]); the stock Kensei K2-250 dynos 47 hp crank-equivalent within 3%, peaking inside 7,000-10,500 rpm; a blocked tune name shows as hashes; forged upgrade buys fail. Relies on Part 8 ([366]-[390]) and Part 2 ([76]-[100]).

[494] Phase 10 - Game modes, races, stunts and leaderboards. Deliver `RaceService` (lobbies, countdown, `Checkpoint` validation, PI class limits, results, `Race_Join`, private lobbies via `TeleportService:ReserveServer` and `TeleportAsync` with `TeleportOptions.ReservedServerAccessCode` per [415]); `EventService`; `Config/Races`; the Skyline Airfield drag strip with a full tree on the 1/4 and 1/8 mile; stunt and FMX scoring; time trials with ghosts; OrderedDataStore leaderboards; every web call through `Util.retryAsync`. The server calls `RequestStreamAroundAsync` for every racer before the countdown. The user places `Checkpoint` parts along the [391]-[415] routes. Acceptance test: a 4-client race reports the correct finishing order with times to 0.001 s; a skipped checkpoint invalidates the lap; the stock Kensei Hayate 1300 runs the 1/4 mile (1,437 studs) within 5% of the [383] estimate of 9.9 s; private lobby teleports succeed in a published test, since Studio cannot teleport. Relies on Part 9 ([391]-[415]).

[495] Phase 11 - Traffic, pedestrians and police. Deliver `TrafficService` (per-region density targets, deterministic spawn seeds, a light cycle derived from `workspace:GetServerTimeNow()`); `TrafficController` simulating cars on the road graph entirely on each client with lane following, car-following, signal obedience at `TrafficLight` tags and yielding per [416]-[430]; pooled car and pedestrian rigs; opt-in police heat; NPC riders; wildlife. The user confirms `TrafficLight` tags and crosswalk markers at every signalized junction. Acceptance test: all clients show the same light phase within 0.2 s; cars spawn 350-600 studs from the camera (150-600 just after a teleport) and never inside the view cone ([423]); traffic sends zero per-car server remotes; `TrafficController` stays within its Medium 1.5 ms `FrameBudget_ms` on a mid-range phone; police only pursue opted-in players; density stays inside the [416]-[430] budgets. Relies on Part 9 ([416]-[430]) and Part 6 ([271]-[295]).

[496] Phase 12 - UI polish, settings, social, monetization, safety and anti-exploit. Deliver `HUDController`, `UIController`, `PhoneController` apps and `SettingsController` (rebinding, assists, MPH/KM/H, quality, accessibility); `CrewService` with filtered names; TextChatService channels; `MonetizationService` for VIP, Garage+, Pro Paint Lab, Lights Pack, 2x Cash and Cash bundles with an idempotent `ProcessReceipt` backed by a purchase ledger; `AntiCheatService` (speed rule, position-jump checks, remote rate limits); admin tools using `Players:BanAsync`; `AnalyticsService` funnels. In Studio the user creates passes and products in Creator Dashboard and pastes their IDs into `Config/Economy.Monetization` ([451]-[475]). Acceptance test: replaying one receipt twice grants Cash once; a client forcing 400 km/h on the Volt Wasp X is corrected and flagged within 2.5 s; settings persist across a rejoin; every menu works with a gamepad alone. Relies on Part 9 ([431]-[450]) and Part 10 ([451]-[475]).

[497] Phase 13 - Optimization, QA and launch. Deliver `debug.profilebegin` labels on every loop, the `/perf` overlay, a pooling and budget audit enforcing [479]-[480], every `/selftest` spec passing, live error monitoring, and a completed launch checklist. The user runs the [481]-[482] test matrix, profiles with the tools in [478], publishes to the private beta, answers the questionnaire and renders the icon and thumbnails. Acceptance test: every [476] target is measured and recorded on a mid-range PC, a real mid-range phone and a console or its emulator; a 30-minute 4-client soak keeps `LuaHeap` and instance counts flat within 5% with zero errors; DevTools `/stress 26` plus 4 clients, then a 20-30 player beta session, hold server heartbeat at 60; every migration fixture passes; [483]-[484] are fully checked off. [498] Extensibility recipes, so post-launch content never touches engine code. New vehicle: add `Config/Vehicles/<VehicleId>.luau` (twelve groups, SI suffixes, fictional names, [37]-[39]), its `AssetIds` and `SoundIds` keys at 0, a [126] stat block in the delivery notes and optionally a MeshPart model ([206]); dealerships, unlocks, PI, race eligibility and Build Sheets pick it up from data. New part: one `Config/Upgrades.Parts` record ([368]). New job: a `Config/Jobs` record, tagged `JobPickup`/`JobDropoff` sites and an `/econsim` pass ([346]). New race: a `Config/Races` entry and `Checkpoint` parts with `Index` and `Respawn` ([392]). New region feature: a folder under `Workspace/World/<Region>` from the [294] kits, zones per [296] and a `RoadGraph.validate()` rerun ([283]). Each recipe ends with `ConfigValidator`, `/selftest` and the touched phase's checklist, delivered as whole files per [9]; a recipe that needs a Service change is a new system and follows [5].

[499] Final checklist; run it before every message containing code. Every script starts with `--!strict` and types its exports. A whole-word search for each [44] banned token, `_G`, `getfenv`, `setfenv` and two-argument `Instance.new` finds nothing outside comments and `task.` calls. Conversion factors such as `21.952` appear only in `Units.luau`; config numbers carry unit suffixes. Asset IDs are `0` in `Config/AssetIds` or `Config/SoundIds`. No real brand, model or logo appears in code, strings, comments or asset names ([19]). Vehicle forces run only in the owning client's `PreSimulation`; every remote comes from `NetDefs`, passes `Net.T` and domain checks, and none uses `InvokeClient`. Saves use ProfileStore, web calls `Util.retryAsync`, player text the [467] filter. No paid randomness, pay-only advantage or non-Mild content. Studio-only properties appear only as MANUAL STEPs. Files are whole, one phase per reply, status "Not run in Studio" ([12]).
